# Current compatibility register and historical audit

The default is the selected EmDash behavior on both self-hosted Node and Cloudflare. A passing scaffold build does not establish either hosting target. Keep differences visible and correct accidental divergence before widening the CMS surface.

## Current landed contracts

This index was checked against CMS main `9bfd6afb044e50c43b3dc5417b2fa04da20dd1bc` for the documentation corrections below; historical evidence retains its own exact heads. Source authority is [EmDash 1.1.0](README.md), immutable pin `913cb1bb9b7f08c3ff0d258b4420e53835b6a58e`. The feature documents and assertion ledgers below retain detailed evidence; this index separates intentional substitutions/bounds, fidelity repairs and unimplemented scope. A merged PR establishes landed state, not specific approval of a deviation. The cited records do not document a specific acceptance decision for the intentional differences indexed here. Divergent assertions are not upstream passes.

### Auth foundation: landed in [PR #5](https://github.com/sveltery/cms/pull/5)

See [auth contract and substitutions](../../docs/auth-foundation.md) and [source/assertion ledger](../../docs/auth-ports.json). These remain bounded resolver contracts without token issuance. PR #7 subsequently added a configurable request handle and migration registration; its production factory remains unconfigured. See the composition entries below.

| ID/source at the EmDash pin | Observable upstream versus local behavior | Rationale and test evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| C-01: cookie/store substitution; [Astro auth middleware](https://github.com/emdash-cms/emdash/blob/913cb1bb9b7f08c3ff0d258b4420e53835b6a58e/packages/core/src/astro/middleware/auth.ts) | Upstream reloads the user from ID-only Astro server-session data. Local `cms-session` cookie resolves through an explicit hash-keyed store, reloading current role/disabled state; `_cms_auth_*` descriptors replace framework storage. | SvelteKit owns application sessions. [SQLite storage tests](../../tests/auth-storage.test.ts) cover hash-only rows, current-role changes, revocation and reopen. PR #7 now registers atomic auth migrations; Production D1/KV composition remains deferred; bounded local D1 storage landed in PR #10 below. |
| C-02: token shape; [tokens.ts](https://github.com/emdash-cms/emdash/blob/913cb1bb9b7f08c3ff0d258b4420e53835b6a58e/packages/auth/src/tokens.ts) | Inspected Astro framework cookies are UUIDs; upstream `generateSessionId` uses 20 random bytes and generic `hashToken` accepts padding/malformed encoding. Local resolver accepts only canonical base64url of 32 bytes and stores decoded-byte SHA-256/base64url hashes. | Reject ambiguous/oversized input before storage. [Session tests](../../tests/auth-session.test.ts), source/runtime identities in the auth ledger. No cookie-byte or production issuance equivalence is claimed. |
| C-03: uncomposed exact-origin helper; [checkPublicCsrf](https://github.com/emdash-cms/emdash/blob/913cb1bb9b7f08c3ff0d258b4420e53835b6a58e/packages/core/src/api/csrf.ts) | Upstream allows absent Origin and `X-EmDash-Request: 1` bypass, or internal/public origin. When explicitly called, local `requireSessionMutationOrigin` requires exact trusted public origin for non-safe methods, including scheme/port, with no bypass; safe methods pass. The helper has no production caller and does not protect every local unsafe request. | Intended session-mutation boundary. [Request tests](../../tests/auth-request.test.ts) cover foreign/missing/opaque/malformed origins and header bypass denial. PR #7’s handle does not compose this helper; native Kit remotes retain their independent origin protection. Logout, renewal, issuance and custom session-CSRF wiring remain deferred. |
| C-04: exact expiry; upstream auth config and inspected Astro runtime in [auth provenance](../../docs/auth-foundation.md) | Upstream config declares sliding/30-day defaults without inspected route-to-TTL wiring; Astro 7.3.2 expires when stored expiry is less than current time. Local absolute expiry rejects equality and checks time after awaited reads, without renewal. | Deterministic portable boundary. [Storage tests](../../tests/auth-storage.test.ts) cover equality and expiry during a pending lookup. No sliding/30-day equivalence or rotation/renewal implementation is claimed. |

Decision status C-01–C-04: landed with PR #5 at merge `2bccff66f94df02e85a96201a24970d541f2ea72`; specific deviation acceptance is not recorded in cited evidence. [Passing implementation-head CI](https://github.com/sveltery/cms/actions/runs/36911991335) at `5d25c84e244c8f22ef3bb7febb0ed5e2139b8dcc` covers the bounded slice. Workerd evidence uses a synthetic store; it does not establish D1 persistence.

### Database slice: landed in [PR #3](https://github.com/sveltery/cms/pull/3)

See [database parity/bounds](../../docs/database-parity.md) and [source ledger](../../docs/database-ports.json). Those documents preserve source IDs, predecessor-pin history and the selected 31 adapted cases.

| ID/source at the EmDash pin | Observable upstream versus local behavior | Rationale and test evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| C-05: storage namespace and bounds; [schema registry](https://github.com/emdash-cms/emdash/blob/913cb1bb9b7f08c3ff0d258b4420e53835b6a58e/packages/core/src/schema/registry.ts), [content repository](https://github.com/emdash-cms/emdash/blob/913cb1bb9b7f08c3ff0d258b4420e53835b6a58e/packages/core/src/database/repositories/content.ts) | Upstream has the wider schema/content model and `_emdash_*` tables. Local uses `_cms_*` system tables with `ec_*` physical collections; limits 100 collections, 32 fields, text 100,000 characters and payload 200,000; strings default to 200. Body-free summaries cap titles at 200; page 50/max 100 matches the selected upstream page bounds, with only created_at/id sorting exposed. | Bound the initial persisted slice and avoid implying an EmDash database upgrade. [Database supplements](../../tests/database.test.ts) cover capacity, field bounds, summaries and cursor filtering. Unsupported types and custom/published sorting are incomplete scope. |
| C-06: mandatory revision preconditions; same content repository | Some upstream low-level writes allow omitted preconditions. Local public service requires `{ version, updatedAt }` on update/delete and expected schema version for additive fields; SQL CAS rejects stale writes without partial changes. | Explicit concurrent-edit boundary. [Database tests](../../tests/database.test.ts) cover two-connection CAS and schema-version conflicts. Published/live revision pointers, history and edit locks remain unimplemented. |
| C-07: atomic write seam; [withTransaction](https://github.com/emdash-cms/emdash/blob/913cb1bb9b7f08c3ff0d258b4420e53835b6a58e/packages/core/src/database/transaction.ts) | Upstream can run a callback directly when transactions are unsupported, losing multi-statement atomicity. Local schema/guarded writes require an adapter-proven atomic batch with no non-atomic fallback; SQLite uses mutex-owned BEGIN IMMEDIATE/COMMIT. | Preserve rollback guarantees without claiming unsupported D1 safety. [Database tests](../../tests/database.test.ts) cover DDL/DML rollback and missing migration tables. Bounded local D1 batches landed in PR #10 below; deployed support remains blocked. |

Decision status C-05–C-07: landed with PR #3 at merge `ec0376fc4d83ba70ec896e6cb34e5ad301f27788`; specific deviation acceptance is not recorded in cited evidence. [Final implementation-head CI](https://github.com/sveltery/cms/actions/runs/36907254702) at `58401b40a10e6602fd91f20456a421434368a9d6` is bounded Node evidence, not deployed runtime parity.

### Content remotes: landed in [PR #6](https://github.com/sveltery/cms/pull/6)

See [native contracts](../../docs/content-remotes.md), [review/evidence](../../docs/content-remote-review.md) and [assertion ledger](../../docs/content-remote-ports.json). Source: pinned [content handlers](https://github.com/emdash-cms/emdash/blob/913cb1bb9b7f08c3ff0d258b4420e53835b6a58e/packages/core/src/api/handlers/content.ts) and repository linked above.

| ID | Observable upstream versus local behavior | Rationale and test evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| C-08: transport and errors | Astro REST/MCP calls become registered Kit queries/forms; bounded JSON `data` handles nullable values and keys colliding with Kit nested form guards. Schema-invalid forms yield Kit field issues; domain errors map to 401/403/404/400/409 envelopes, `FORBIDDEN` to `INSUFFICIENT_PERMISSIONS`, and expected UNIQUE errors to conflicts. Unexpected errors stay sanitized. | Native framework encoding and explicit domain mapping. [Production remote tests](../../tests/production/remotes.test.ts) and [mutations](../../tests/production/mutations.test.ts) exercise real registration, guarded keys, errors and no-write denials. Framework envelope agreement is supplemental, not whole-source parity. |
| C-09: revision transport | Upstream revision checks use version/updatedAt; local `_rev` encodes that pair plus collection/locale/entry context and hides numeric storage version. Malformed or context-mismatched tokens return 400; stale valid tokens return 409, without partial writes. | Opaque client round trips while preserving storage CAS; token is neither credential nor edit lock. [Mutation tests](../../tests/production/mutations.test.ts) and source ledger preserve selected scalar assertions; published lifecycle remains unported. |
| C-10: receipts and refresh | Upstream content operations can return content records. Local create/update returns only identity/revision receipts so write-only callers receive no stored fields. Separate canonical query refreshes preserve read errors/native keys, limited to five requested lists and five details in the changed scope. | Keep mutation success from disclosing data through denied reads. [Ownership tests](../../tests/production/ownership.test.ts), production mutations and review reproducers cover receipts, native async refreshes and limits. |

Decision status C-08–C-10: landed with PR #6 at merge `8bd3e62fe131116670fea7b87e623ba30fd84b19`; specific deviation acceptance is not recorded in cited evidence. [CI at implementation checkpoint `f1d3a22`](https://github.com/sveltery/cms/actions/runs/36912708457) predates the final null-preview correction; it is not final-head evidence for that correction.

The merged null-preview fix at `066e1879ae21b0d891b69627ec8138da77243cc5` now has [executable pinned rendering evidence](../../docs/preview-upstream-reproduction.md): 28 paired string/text cases render the unchanged EmDash editor/real controls and local before/fix/current sources. Explicit null changes locally from the supplied default to empty, matching EmDash: this is a fidelity repair at that direct-control boundary, not a shared upstream defect. Projected descriptors omit defaults, so projected null was already empty before the fix. Browser interactions, database/API default application and whole-editor parity remain unverified.

| ID/source | Observable upstream versus local behavior | Rationale, evidence and decision status |
| --- | --- | --- |
| C-16: scalar preview adaptation; pinned [ContentEditor](https://github.com/emdash-cms/emdash/blob/913cb1bb9b7f08c3ff0d258b4420e53835b6a58e/packages/admin/src/components/ContentEditor.tsx) and [manifest conversion](https://github.com/emdash-cms/emdash/blob/913cb1bb9b7f08c3ff0d258b4420e53835b6a58e/packages/core/src/api/handlers/manifest.ts) | EmDash descriptors omit defaults and absent values render empty. Local direct callers can supply defaults for absent own keys. The fix also treats inherited keys as absent while EmDash reads inherited strings; runtime false/zero stringify locally versus upstream empty. The actual projected preview receives no defaults. | Default fallback presents local schema metadata; own-key handling reflects persisted JSON records; coercion follows the local scalar controls. [Paired SSR reproduction](../../docs/preview-upstream-reproduction.md) and [local regression](../../tests/draft-preview.test.ts) separate these differences from matching null/string values. Inherited/false/zero are runtime probes, not supported persisted cases or parity credit. Default/coercion predate the fix; own-key handling changed with it. Landed in [PR #6](https://github.com/sveltery/cms/pull/6), merge `8bd3e62fe131116670fea7b87e623ba30fd84b19`; specific deviation acceptance is not recorded. |

### Session/editor composition: landed in [PR #7](https://github.com/sveltery/cms/pull/7)

[The composition contract and differences](../../docs/session-composition.md) and [source/assertion ledger](../../docs/session-composition-ports.json) retain exact source blobs, selected assertion omissions, test-first checkpoints and runtime limits. Decision status C-11–C-15: landed at `a9726b2a67e57290e7c49b1ddb2a1b9b1f4b1ada`; specific deviation acceptance is not recorded in the cited evidence and is not inferred from that merge. [Implementation-head CI](https://github.com/sveltery/cms/actions/runs/36928939940) checked synthetic merge `2a77ec8ba7d25984e3f92ed8bda974ef17564aee`, containing head `d875a560ae0aed37e8549ae876131ebb49b958d6` and main `8bd3e62`. It is not post-merge execution evidence.

| ID/source at the EmDash pin | Observable upstream versus local behavior | Rationale and evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| C-11: request/permission composition; [auth middleware](https://github.com/emdash-cms/emdash/blob/913cb1bb9b7f08c3ff0d258b4420e53835b6a58e/packages/core/src/astro/middleware/auth.ts) | Astro middleware becomes a request-scoped Kit handle that reloads cookie-backed current identity/role and projects exactly nine server-derived supported permissions. Earlier cookie/hash/expiry substitutions remain C-01–C-04. Production hook installs an unconfigured factory. | Native composition without granting client claims or unsupported capabilities. [Composition tests](../../tests/session-composition.test.ts), [production tests](../../tests/production/session-composition.test.ts), synthetic workerd evidence in the ledger. No production login/session issuance or D1 guarantee. |
| C-12: migrations; [upstream auth migration](https://github.com/emdash-cms/emdash/blob/913cb1bb9b7f08c3ff0d258b4420e53835b6a58e/packages/core/src/database/migrations/008_auth.ts) and migration-race source IDs in the ledger | Full upstream users/passkeys/accounts migrations become independent minimal auth tables in an atomic version-two upgrade. Upstream accepts a future-version fast path; local rejects unknown versions and malformed known auth DDL/index without repair. | Independent known-schema boundary. [Migration tests](../../tests/session-migrations.test.ts) cover clean/upgrade/restart/concurrency/rollback and the local snapshot-race fix. No EmDash import, universal schema checksum or upstream-defect claim. |
| C-13: mutation gate; pinned content routes linked in the composition ledger | Upstream authorizes content writes by permission; local authenticated HTTP mutations additionally require trusted `mutationsEnabled: true`, defaulting to 503 before storage. Anonymous requests retain 401; direct trusted service calls remain explicit server APIs. | Keep HTTP writes disabled during bounded integration. Production native-form/gate tests verify denial and zero writes. UI controls stay disabled; this does not implement enabled editing. |
| C-14: manifest access/projection; [manifest route](https://github.com/emdash-cms/emdash/blob/913cb1bb9b7f08c3ff0d258b4420e53835b6a58e/packages/core/src/astro/routes/api/manifest.ts), [builder](https://github.com/emdash-cms/emdash/blob/913cb1bb9b7f08c3ff0d258b4420e53835b6a58e/packages/core/src/api/handlers/manifest.ts) | Upstream authenticated manifest becomes `getEditorManifest`, requiring content-read and draft-read; subscribers receive 403 while contributors/authors get scalar metadata without administrative schema-read. Projection omits full manifest/hash/config extras, defaults and storage metadata; text retains richText kind in a disabled plain textarea. | Bounded disclosure and framework adaptation. [Manifest tests](../../tests/editor-manifest.test.ts) and production tests preserve selected empty/fresh/scalar validation assertions, with integer/richer fields unported. Both inspected descriptor builders omit database defaults. |
| C-15: manifest query/failure model; same builder and ledger, [pinned registry:418](https://github.com/emdash-cms/emdash/blob/913cb1bb9b7f08c3ff0d258b4420e53835b6a58e/packages/core/src/schema/registry.ts#L418) | Upstream uses one collection query plus field queries in batches of 50 IDs, rather than a JOIN or universal two-query guarantee. Proposed [bounded batching](../../docs/editor-manifest-batching.md) replaces local N+1 reads with at most three queries under the existing 100-collection cap. Database errors still propagate because this dashboard-stored model has no config source; React/Astro consumption remains native Kit queries in disabled previews. | Preserve field/collection caps, existing order, disclosure, malformed-JSON errors and fresh reads. Source five-collection assertions are output evidence; 0/1/50/51/100 query counts and Node/localD1 comparisons are supplemental. No config-fallback, snapshot or full-manifest parity. C-15's original adaptation landed in PR #7; this read optimization remains proposed, with no specific historical deviation acceptance inferred. |

The pinned manual `constructor` collection omission is **retained**, not corrected: [CMS issue #8](https://github.com/sveltery/cms/issues/8) tracks later work. The composition document records isolated execution of the pinned builder and the local regression. Own-key lookup safely renders omitted descriptors unavailable while administrative storage/reads remain intact. This limitation is separate from the already-fixed null-preview correction and receives no additional parity credit.

Configured production authentication/storage, login/provisioning, enabled editor writes, production Cloudflare/D1 composition, configured hosting, richer fields, publication/revisions, published-content restore/permanent deletion and configured schema dashboard writes remain incomplete. Indexing these contracts changes no product behavior or inventory credit. Keep [MIT attribution](../../notices/emdash-MIT.txt); track newly verified shared bugs in this repository's GitHub issues for deferred work. Already-fixed contracts do not require duplicate issues.

## Bounded D1 adapter: landed in PR #10

See [the D1 contract/evidence](../../docs/d1-database.md) and [separate source ledger](../../docs/d1-ports.json). Source authority remains EmDash 1.1.0 `913cb1bb9b7f08c3ff0d258b4420e53835b6a58e`, [dialect](https://github.com/emdash-cms/emdash/blob/913cb1bb9b7f08c3ff0d258b4420e53835b6a58e/packages/cloudflare/src/db/d1-dialect.ts) and [tests](https://github.com/emdash-cms/emdash/blob/913cb1bb9b7f08c3ff0d258b4420e53835b6a58e/packages/cloudflare/tests/db/d1-dialect.test.ts). Six selected raw-binding cases pass; parameter/result differences below receive no cross-runtime parity credit. Local workerd/D1 evidence supersedes the historical blocked raw-adapter statements, not the production hosting or full EmDash limits.

| ID | Observable upstream versus local behavior | Rationale, evidence and status |
| --- | --- | --- |
| C-23: bounded Kysely/D1 seam | Upstream RawBindingD1Dialect extends kysely-d1 and the row-locking adapter, provides wider introspection and `executeAtomicBatch`. Local structural driver uses the existing Kysely SQLite compiler, exposes CmsDatabase.atomicBatch with the same one-binding-batch forwarding and raw concurrency, rejects general introspection/Kysely migrations, callback transactions and streaming. Sessions/coalescing are unimplemented. | Bounded adapter through the existing CMS seam; no new dependency or general migration runner. Six raw-binding source assertions, actual local DDL/DML rollback and shared CMS contract pass. [Upstream reproducer](../../scripts/reproduce-d1-upstream.mjs) executes the immutable source. Landed in PR #10; specific deviation acceptance not recorded. |
| C-17: result/parameter/runtime metadata | Upstream and local D1 preserve RETURNING rows and map zero affected rows to undefined; Node uses 0n for non-returning zero writes and omits metadata for RETURNING. Upstream ordinary queries also expose deprecated numUpdatedOrDeletedRows; local queries omit it under the existing Kysely 0.29.2 QueryResult seam, matching upstream batches. D1 rejects undefined/bigint accepted by Node; accepts ArrayBuffer/byte arrays rejected by Node and returns BLOB arrays. Local D1 creates _cf_METADATA on its first failed write; Node does not. | Retain supported pinned D1 mapping, no unrequested normalization. CMS does not consume the deprecated alias. Real local D1/Node comparisons and isolated upstream reproduction assert alias presence/absence, support/rejections and application rollback excluding exactly platform-owned metadata. Other variants and live limits untested. Landed in PR #10; no metadata/unsupported-variant parity credit or specific acceptance decision recorded. |
| C-18: CMS migration race envelope classification | Upstream has its wider EmDash row-lock/migration model. This CMS retains its own atomic fresh/v1→v2 auth migrations; Node-only exact error comparisons now also recognize measured anchored D1 SQLite envelopes, recovering only known races after proving complete v2. Unexpected failures still propagate. | Fidelity repair to local adapter portability, not an upstream bug fix. Actual forced D1 migration/same-slug races, snapshot upgrade, failed DDL/marker rollback and negative envelope tests pass. No repair of partial/future schemas, general retries or interrupted-request guarantee. Landed in PR #10; earlier C-12 schema substitution remains intentional with acceptance not recorded. |

Landed in [PR #10](https://github.com/sveltery/cms/pull/10) at merge `30ba4831cd8f402541a46161a151271fa650b213`. The former duplicate C-16 identifier for the D1 seam is now C-23; scalar preview remains C-16. This identifier/status repair changes no runtime behavior, assertions or parity credit, and does not establish specific acceptance of the D1 differences. No newly verified shared upstream bug was identified. Production factories/credentials/admin setup, the default-disabled mutation gate and UI controls remain unchanged. Live D1, Cloudflare hosting/configured deployment, login/provisioning, sessions/bookmarks, general migration/introspection and richer content lifecycle remain incomplete.

The original PR handoff records its implementation-head CI and independent/configured review; those results do not establish deployed availability. ## Session/permission implementation refinements: landed in PR #13

Source authority is EmDash 1.1.0 `913cb1bb9b7f08c3ff0d258b4420e53835b6a58e`: [tokens](https://github.com/emdash-cms/emdash/blob/913cb1bb9b7f08c3ff0d258b4420e53835b6a58e/packages/auth/src/tokens.ts), [session resolver](https://github.com/emdash-cms/emdash/blob/913cb1bb9b7f08c3ff0d258b4420e53835b6a58e/packages/core/src/astro/session-user.ts), and [RBAC](https://github.com/emdash-cms/emdash/blob/913cb1bb9b7f08c3ff0d258b4420e53835b6a58e/packages/auth/src/rbac.ts).

| ID | Existing upstream/local contract and implementation refinement | Rationale, evidence and status |
| --- | --- | --- |
| C-21: validated session bytes | Upstream generic hashing accepts noncanonical encoding; local C-02 validation remains stricter. Local resolution retains already-validated bytes and privately hashes them instead of invoking validation twice. Public hashing and revocation validation, digest timeout/keepAlive, current stored role and exact expiry remain unchanged. | Remove redundant work inside the portable auth module. [Auth feature record](../../docs/auth-foundation.md), supplemental canonical-bit/digest-timeout [tests](../../tests/auth-session.test.ts); original source assertions remain. Landed in [PR #13](https://github.com/sveltery/cms/pull/13) at merge `ab8a1889b9750e617113d98bf1dd3a591f925207`. |
| C-22: internal mutation authorization | Pinned own/any and nonempty-owner policy remains. The local service checks general mutation permission once before parsing, passes the copied actor to its stored-owner helper and retains repository owner predicates/CAS. No intended error-order or persisted behavior difference. | Remove repeated private checks without weakening a public boundary. [Database feature record](../../docs/database.md), hostile-input/no-storage [tests](../../tests/database.test.ts), existing ownership/concurrency and production tests. Landed in [PR #13](https://github.com/sveltery/cms/pull/13) at merge `ab8a1889b9750e617113d98bf1dd3a591f925207`. |

The user authorized these audited implementation refinements on 2026-10-02. Their landed status and the earlier C-02/C-06 specific-deviation acceptance records remain separate; no new upstream assertion credit or production composition is claimed. Exact-head CI and independent/configured review belong to the PR handoff.

### Proposed native UI/config refinements

Source authority remains EmDash 1.1.0 `913cb1bb9b7f08c3ff0d258b4420e53835b6a58e`: [editor](https://github.com/emdash-cms/emdash/blob/913cb1bb9b7f08c3ff0d258b4420e53835b6a58e/packages/admin/src/components/ContentEditor.tsx), [manifest](https://github.com/emdash-cms/emdash/blob/913cb1bb9b7f08c3ff0d258b4420e53835b6a58e/packages/core/src/api/handlers/manifest.ts), [content handlers](https://github.com/emdash-cms/emdash/blob/913cb1bb9b7f08c3ff0d258b4420e53835b6a58e/packages/core/src/api/handlers/content.ts), and [Node starter](https://github.com/emdash-cms/emdash/blob/913cb1bb9b7f08c3ff0d258b4420e53835b6a58e/templates/starter/astro.config.mjs). The user authorized these audited refinements on 2026-10-02. Proposed in [PR #14](https://github.com/sveltery/cms/pull/14), not landed; historical domain-deviation decisions and source assertion credit remain unchanged.

| ID | Upstream/local boundary and proposed difference | Evidence and owning record |
| --- | --- | --- |
| F-04: reactive preview declarations/mapper | React editing descriptors remain a disabled Svelte scalar preview with C-16's disclosed own-key/default/coercion behavior. Block-local modern const declarations use `$derived`, and the shared typed mapper preserves all existing descriptor spread/conversion behavior. No intended display or lifetime difference. | Expanded SSR/mapper and actual client-prop regressions; [remote feature record](../../docs/content-remotes.md). |
| F-05: native refresh lifecycle | Astro content writes remain Kit forms with C-10 bounded receipts/query refreshes. Replace redundant returned-Promise collection with Kit's eager refresh registration; preserve limits, scope, aliases, native keys and separate query errors. | Registered production refresh/filter/error tests and [remote feature record](../../docs/content-remotes.md). |
| F-06: one Kit configuration entry | Astro standalone/config becomes the existing explicit/default Kit adapters. Same preprocessing/compiler/remote options move into the supported flat Vite plugin API; old config is removed. No adapter, hosting or security/lifecycle difference. | Default build plus isolated Node package/bootstrap and hosted jobs; [Node feature record](../../docs/node-hosting.md). |
| F-07: framework path resolution | Existing Svelte routes manually assembled root-absolute links. Kit `resolve` retains supported empty-base targets, can render relative SSR hrefs, encodes route parameters and prefixes a configured app base. Nonempty-base home/content navigation intentionally stays within the app base. Portable UI gets ordinary href props. | URL-resolved production assertions, client navigation/reused parameters and isolated `/cms` browser tests; [remote feature record](../../docs/content-remotes.md). |

Full final-head bootstrap, secured hosted browser checks and independent/configured review are required before merge. A local browser installation failure is not browser evidence. No new production session/storage composition, enabled editing or deployment is claimed.

These substitutions describe the framework integration direction; they do not establish acceptance of changes to domain defaults or policy. Native transport failures need an explicit mapping to upstream validation/auth/not-found/conflict results. Keep error details that affect callers; a SvelteKit response shape alone is not a reason to weaken an assertion.

## Foundation audit at `ac0ec3f`

These findings describe the original provisional scaffold at `ac0ec3f`; they are a historical audit, not a claim that every finding remains open on current main. Foundation subsequently landed at `89a1663df69d3e788e2290254b7a54b30b071ded` with registered fail-closed remotes, supplemental service/production/browser checks and updated architecture guidance. Its fixtures still do not establish real-session CRUD, provider persistence or executable EmDash parity. The UI is disabled, there is no trusted session/storage wiring, and no exploit or functioning persisted CMS is claimed. Implementation owners must resolve and track remaining differences; this documentation PR leaves their files untouched.

| ID | Foundation location and concrete trigger | Observable difference and upstream anchor | Disposition |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| A-01 | `src/lib/server/content/schema.ts:4`: create data lacking `title`, containing a collection-specific field, or title with surrounding spaces | Fixed required title/body and trimming constrain dashboard-defined collection data. Upstream registry and generated validators use persisted fields; handler supports no title/name and returns null slug. | Correct before persisted schema/content slice. Validate against the stored collection; preserve source rules rather than imposing scaffold normalization. |
| A-02 | `src/lib/server/content/service.ts:5` and `service.ts:42`: a write principal updates/deletes another author's record | Only global read/write capabilities exist; record has no owner/role/publish distinction. Auth RBAC distinguishes own/any, drafts, create/publish/permanent-delete and schema management. Null owner does not grant ownership. | Correct before exposing writes. Worker policy contract must include trusted role/owner checks; denial precedes mutation. |
| A-03 | `src/lib/server/content/service.ts:8`: two collections need different fields, or a draft of published content is saved | Record shape has only id/title/body; no collection, arbitrary data, status, locale, draft/live pointers, timestamps or revision condition. MCP draft tests preserve live data and merge partial changes into drafts. | Correct with source-backed persisted model; do not migrate the provisional shape as the final schema. |
| A-04 | `src/lib/server/content/service.ts:14` and `service.ts:49`: user presses delete | Repository delete returns only boolean; no trash/restore/permanent-delete distinction. A future physical-delete implementation could lose the recoverable deletion behavior. | Unspecified behavior, not proven data loss. Establish trash semantics and permanent-delete guard tests before adapter implementation. |
| A-05 | `service.ts:13` and `schema.ts:8`: two editors submit the same previously read record | No `_rev` input/output or conditional write exists. Upstream publish and draft-save races require conflict/no partial mutation; public `updatedAt` does not stand in for version. | Correct before concurrent editing; retain edit-lock and CAS as separate protections. |
| A-06 | `svelte.config.js:1`, README, `docs/architecture.md`: advertise Node/Cloudflare parity after adapter-auto build | `adapter-auto` and absent persistence/runtime bindings prove neither target; “Cloudflare-first” and unresolved storage/auth decisions are stale against the requested two-target port. | Parent/adapter owner must update architecture and README and prove both targets. Do not count a generic build as adapter evidence. |
| A-07 | README and `docs/architecture.md`: follow “no compatibility promised” / “original implementation only” guidance while porting tests | Guidance predates explicit behavior fidelity and licensed test copying. It would permit arbitrary differences or omit attribution when test excerpts are copied. | This directory records the current mandate and upstream notice. Parent should reconcile top-level documentation in its owned branch. |
| A-08 | `tests/content.test.ts`: pass all three scaffold tests | A call-count fake checks authorization/validation only. It cannot prove database-defined schema, persistence, browser/remote registration, ownership, draft/trash, concurrency or either adapter. | Retain useful original denial tests; add upstream ports first and record red/green runs against actual product boundaries. |

## Historical scaffold-era decision statement and current policy

The scaffold-era planning PR recorded **no accepted domain behavior deviations**. That historical statement is not a claim that current main has no intentional differences; the landed contracts above now provide the current index. A different runtime transaction API, native remote envelope, or absent Astro type generator needs adaptation evidence; it does not automatically authorize a behavior change.

For each proposed deviation add: stable ID; exact upstream source IDs and assertions; baseline behavior; proposed observable difference; documented SvelteKit/Node/Cloudflare constraint with authoritative source; alternatives considered; affected runtimes/users/data; truthful decision status, landed PR; paired regression tests; migration and documentation requirements. Keep proposed, landed and specifically accepted statuses separate, with evidence for each transition. “Simpler”, “Cloudflare-first”, “temporary UI”, or “tests pass locally” is not a platform constraint. A deferred feature is incomplete scope and remains blocked, rather than becoming an accepted deviation.

## Proposed collection metadata update service

[PR #16](https://github.com/sveltery/cms/pull/16), implementation head `64a69cf584ac834a596675fef8891291f004be7b`, adds bounded existing-column metadata updates against EmDash 1.1.0 immutable `913cb1bb9b7f08c3ff0d258b4420e53835b6a58e`. The [detailed feature/source/fixture record](../../docs/collection-update.md) records all five assertions at registry.test.ts:234/:248/:336, executed assertion red/green evidence, source blobs and omissions.

| Proposed record | Observable upstream / local behavior | Rationale, evidence and decision status |
| --- | --- | --- |
| CU-01: metadata validation, flags and nullable projection; pinned registry `updateCollection`/`mapCollectionRow`, [types](https://github.com/emdash-cms/emdash/blob/913cb1bb9b7f08c3ff0d258b4420e53835b6a58e/packages/core/src/schema/types.ts) | Upstream low-level registry accepts raw whitespace/empty labels and six support flags (`drafts`, `revisions`, `preview`, `scheduling`, `search`, `seo`); local reuses trimmed nonempty label/bounds, strict input and only existing draft/revision flags. Upstream omitted nullable metadata maps to undefined; local retains its existing null projection. | Bounded existing storage/service contract. Paired upstream raw-label probes and local validation/no-storage tests measure the difference; no validation/projection parity credit. Proposed PR, specific acceptance not recorded. |
| CU-02: timestamp CAS, atomic receipt and error mapping; [upstream registry](https://github.com/emdash-cms/emdash/blob/913cb1bb9b7f08c3ff0d258b4420e53835b6a58e/packages/core/src/schema/registry.ts#L1077) / [transaction helper](https://github.com/emdash-cms/emdash/blob/913cb1bb9b7f08c3ff0d258b4420e53835b6a58e/packages/core/src/database/transaction.ts) | Upstream takes no update precondition, writes `new Date().toISOString()` (same-clock updates can share a timestamp), reselects the row, and falls back to separate D1 statements. Local trusted service requires version/timestamp; registry guards observed state atomically, uses `max(Date.now(), previous timestamp + 1 ms)`, returns its own `RETURNING` row and maps a missing collection to existing `NOT_FOUND` rather than upstream `COLLECTION_NOT_FOUND`. Both preserve supplied/omitted metadata and do not increment schema version. | Keep existing CAS/atomic adapter policy effective for metadata and backward/equal clocks without inventing a schema-version bump. Frozen-clock, stale/schema/racing guard, no-write/rollback and returned-row tests pass on Node/local D1. Upstream same-clock probe confirms the timestamp difference. Proposed PR, specific acceptance not recorded. |

Evidence: [local selected cases](../../tests/collection-update-upstream.test.ts), [CAS/no-write/authorization/restart](../../tests/collection-update.test.ts), [in-workerd D1](../../tests/collection-update-workerd.test.ts), and [complete-source paired reproducer](../../scripts/reproduce-collection-update-upstream.mjs). [CI run 36977195941](https://github.com/sveltery/cms/actions/runs/36977195941) passes bootstrap and sandboxed default/Node Chromium on that exact head. Independent GPT-6.1 Sol high review found no material implementation findings. The configured ready-triggered review requested this canonical registration; fresh review/checks are required if integration changes the head. Other collection metadata/subsystems remain unimplemented; production mutations stay disabled, with no deployed parity or specific deviation acceptance claimed.

## Proposed bounded schema administration — [PR #18](https://github.com/sveltery/cms/pull/18)

Source authority is EmDash 1.1.0 immutable `913cb1bb9b7f08c3ff0d258b4420e53835b6a58e`; the [feature/source/fixture record](../../docs/schema-admin.md) and [assertion ledger](../../docs/schema-admin-ports.json) contain exact pinned handlers, collection routes, request schemas, registry tests and admin sources. All 25 selected registry assertions from 11 declarations pass separately on Node and local D1. Registered native-form, conversion, capability, CAS and consumer-refresh cases are supplemental; they grant no divergent parity credit.

| Record | Observable pinned upstream / local behavior | Rationale and evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| SA-01: framework, transport, receipts and refresh | Astro JSON REST GET/POST/PUT, creation HTTP 201, `{ item }`/`{ items }`, React/TanStack routes and invalidations become native SvelteKit query/forms and `/schema` routes. Collection reads always include fields, while the pinned GET makes them optional. Queries return service definitions; mutations return only slug/version/timestamp or collection/field-slug receipts. Kit validation produces native field issues; domain errors use 401/403/404/400/409 and `FORBIDDEN` becomes `INSUFFICIENT_PERMISSIONS`. Kit owns form encoding, origin checks and error envelopes. Unexpected failures use Kit's sanitized 500 handling rather than upstream `SCHEMA_*_ERROR` results. Local UI differences are described in the linked feature record. Refreshes target the schema list/detail, existing content list/detail and editor manifest. | Supported Svelte 5.57.1/Kit 2.70.3 primitives and existing service boundaries. [Registered HTTP tests](../../tests/production/schema-admin-remotes.test.ts) exercise the real compiled exports, receipts, field issues, fresh labels/manifests and native forms; these are supplemental framework contracts, not REST/React parity. Native no-JavaScript page responses and JS remote envelopes have different transport status behavior. |
| SA-02: conversion, validation and scalar scope | JSON arrays/booleans/numbers and upstream unknown default values become bounded native flat-form strings, JSON-string supports, checked booleans and canonical decimal versions/lengths. Upstream route label/singular/description bounds differ; its React editor maps empty description to omitted, while an enabled local empty description persists `''`. Upstream schemas strip unknown keys; local strict schemas reject. Local nullable metadata projection remains null rather than upstream undefined. Upstream field requests accept unknown/null defaults and nullable nested validation; local string defaults and flat optional lengths do not reproduce that shape. Only draft/revision flags and string/text fields are available. | Preserve omitted/empty meanings through native controls and existing domain validation. This inherits [C-05 database bounds](../../docs/database-parity.md) and [CU-01 metadata](../../docs/collection-update.md) differences. Framework validation/conversion/no-write cases are supplemental, not divergent upstream passes. |
| SA-03: concurrency and atomicity | Pinned `updateCollection`/`createField` accept no schema-request CAS; upstream's collection mapper exposes no schema version. Local metadata requires version/timestamp CAS and leaves schema version unchanged; fields require schema-version CAS and increment it. Existing adapter-proven atomic batches protect DDL/metadata together; upstream transaction fallback can run separate D1 statements. | Preserve landed [C-06/C-07](../../docs/database-parity.md) and [CU-02](../../docs/collection-update.md) contracts. Stale metadata/field/schema writes and rollback are tested separately from source assertions. No content `_rev`, shared concurrency-bug claim or upstream CAS equivalence. |
| SA-04: permission and configuration precedence | Pinned schema routes call `requireDb` before `requirePerm(schema:read/manage)`. Local checks trusted identity and operation permission before mutation gate/configuration inspection; trusted `mutationsEnabled: true` is required and defaults to disabled 503. Kit may validate native form input before callback authorization; every storage access stays behind permission checks. | Keep production writes disabled and avoid disclosing storage configuration to denied principals. Temporary persisted trusted sessions and SQLite/local D1 fixtures exercise no-write denials. Current roles/permissions come from the existing server resolver; client claims grant nothing. Default/Node production composition stays unconfigured. |
| SA-05: future scalar default/uniqueness fidelity repair | Pinned required string/text addition supplies SQL explicit/default-empty values without implicit metadata defaults. Optional explicit defaults and scalar unique remain metadata only; raw omissions yield NULL and raw/full/partial duplicate values succeed. [PR #21](https://github.com/sveltery/cms/pull/21) restores this for newly created fields. Existing physical defaults/indexes remain divergent pending a separately reviewed upgrade. | [Scalar repair/evidence](../../docs/scalar-field-fidelity.md), paired 24-case Node/local D1 matrix, persisted restart/CAS/independent rollback. Verified inherited unenforced uniqueness is preserved and deferred in [issue #20](https://github.com/sveltery/cms/issues/20). Required-empty-string validation was separately recorded as SF-03 and is now covered by the [bounded validation repair](../../docs/required-scalar-validation.md); the C-07/SA-03 atomicity difference remains unchanged. Proposed repair; no retrofit, specific acceptance or whole-feature parity. |

Status: proposed in [PR #18](https://github.com/sveltery/cms/pull/18), specific deviation acceptance is not recorded. Canonical registration preserves the independent metadata/cursor/manifest records. Production authentication and storage remain unconfigured, and mutations remain default-disabled. Deletion, richer schema metadata/fields, publishing, deployed Cloudflare/D1 and full REST/React/MCP parity remain incomplete scope. Final-head CI, sandboxed default/Node browser acceptance and independent/configured reviews remain mandatory before parent-coordinated merge.

| DT-02: CAS/timestamp/atomic receipt; pinned `ContentRepository.restore` and transaction helper | Upstream allows an optional precondition, observes row version/timestamp, writes current clock and increments version; frozen-clock restores can retain updatedAt. Local requires both tokens, repeats owner predicates, guards schema version atomically, advances timestamp at least 1 ms and returns its own committed row. Upstream active restore conflicts; missing repository result maps to handler `NOT_FOUND`; local preserves those service codes. | Preserve existing mutation/CAS/atomic-adapter policy, persisted ownership and refreshed tokens. Paired frozen-clock probe, Node two-connection CAS/owner/RETURNING races and local D1 CAS/owner races, trigger rollback and restart tests. Different mandatory validation/monotonic timestamp/schema guard contract earns no parity credit. Proposed in PR #19; specific acceptance not recorded. |
| DT-03: validation/error/transport boundary; pinned handler versus local trusted service | Upstream restore handler exposes success/error envelopes, encoded optional `_rev` and ID/slug resolution. Local strict Valibot input accepts bounded type/ID/locale, mandatory version/timestamp, rejects extra identity keys and throws existing `CmsError` codes. Trash reads omit locale across locales; restore defaults `en` as existing local mutations. Read permissions and edit-own/any restoration happen in the service before input/storage, with persisted-owner denial before CAS mutation. | Preserve existing service APIs/error order used by schema-admin; no native transport is added. | DT-04: indexed-search repair and local backfill; pinned `SchemaRegistry.createContentTable`/`ContentRepository.findTrashed` | Upstream's deleted-leading `(deleted_at,status)` index supports global trash search with a final-term tie sort. The original port's locale-leading-only layout scanned and fully sorted; adding the same deleted-leading shape restores measured indexed-search behavior and retains the upstream tie sort. Local explicit migration backfills missing indexes atomically after existing system/auth validation, without changing markers or scalar rules; upstream has a wider index/migration catalog. | Actual immutable registry/repository paired Node/local D1 SQL/plans and zero source assertion credit. Supplemental v1/v2 concurrent installation, wrong-name/preflight races, auth/index batch rollback, idempotence and restart tests. The [paired physical-index probe](../../scripts/reproduce-draft-trash-index-upstream.mjs) verifies that upstream uses a deleted-leading search plus final-term tie sort on Node/local D1. The local full scan was a missing-layout fidelity issue; inherited tie sorting remains a performance limitation, and the no-sort probe alternative is not shipped. The parent authorized the minimal repair after this verification. No live migration is run.

## Existing scalar field label editing

Source authority is EmDash 1.1.0 immutable `913cb1bb9b7f08c3ff0d258b4420e53835b6a58e`; the [feature/source record](../../docs/field-label.md) and [partial assertion ledger](../../docs/field-label-ports.json) distinguish one label assertion from the three-assertion registry declaration at line 668. The pinned reference passed 12 tests (2 partial label runtime cases plus 10 supplemental); local service passed 37 (2 partial runtime cases plus 35 supplemental), and remote/conversion passed 12. Zero complete new source declarations are claimed. Source inspection and supplemental framework/storage tests grant no omitted sort-order/widget or broader-field credit.

| Proposed record | Observable pinned upstream / local behavior | Rationale and evidence status |
| --- | --- | --- |
| FL-01: native transport, strict input and bounded editor | Pinned [admin API](https://github.com/emdash-cms/emdash/blob/913cb1bb9b7f08c3ff0d258b4420e53835b6a58e/packages/admin/src/lib/api/schema.ts#L425) sends JSON PUT and returns a full field from `{ item }`; the local registered Kit form sends native flat form data and a bounded receipt. Upstream allows omitted label and broader edit properties and its object schema strips unknown keys; the local label operation requires label and rejects other properties except the validated, stripped Kit instance key. React/TanStack callbacks and cache invalidation become inline native issues/results and server query refreshes. | Supported Svelte 5.57.1/Kit 2.70.3 APIs and a label-only service boundary. Production/native/enhanced/origin/consumer tests are supplemental framework requirements. The focused production/conversion suite passed 12 cases; hosted sandboxed default/Node browser runs passed 21 cases per target. No REST/React or broader-field parity credit. |
| FL-02: permission, mutation configuration and errors | Pinned schema routes inspect database configuration before permissions. Local checks trusted identity and operation permission before mutation gate/configuration; trusted `mutationsEnabled: true` remains required, defaulting to disabled 503. Kit can validate a form before its callback, while every storage access remains authorized. Missing fields use the existing local `NOT_FOUND` mapping rather than upstream `FIELD_NOT_FOUND`; Kit owns issue/error envelopes and sanitized unexpected 500 responses rather than upstream schema error envelopes. | Preserve the existing request/service authorization policy and avoid disclosing storage configuration to denied callers. No-write denials and mapped failures are supplemental. The focused 37-case service suite and 12-case production/conversion suite passed; inherited principal/auth projection differences retain their own records. |
| FL-03: atomic receipt and subsystem limits | Upstream updates field metadata, reselects the field inside `withTransaction`, invalidates field/media caches and notifies development type generation; D1 can fall back to separate statements. Local obtains its receipt from the atomic update result through the existing adapter contract and refreshes active schema/manifest queries. Neither label-only operation requires CAS or changes collection timestamp/version. Media usage and development type generation are absent local subsystems. | Preserve faithful label values and last-writer-wins while retaining existing atomic persistence/error policy. Two-runtime rollback, target identity, own-receipt, independent-operation and restart tests are supplemental; the focused service suite passed. No full transaction fallback/cache/media/typegen parity. |

Status: implementation landed in [PR #24](https://github.com/sveltery/cms/pull/24) at `1a51b66a5599fcb061940b69b93c2cac8a1fcfa5`, based on main `bf9d1c34cde4bb972b7d2d9a599c16d406607d49` and integrated with trash-view main `06ecd8889d625e24c564e59a8ed5a5e63daa8e8f`; final reviewed head `518ad8ac3246aef8eb358fddd6738d02527e380c` passed [CI](https://github.com/sveltery/cms/actions/runs/36995148922), secured default/Node browsers, and independent/configured reviews. Specific acceptance of differences is not recorded. Label writes faithfully preserve untrimmed nonempty/unbounded values, last-writer-wins and unchanged collection timestamp/version. Existing metadata tokens cannot overwrite labels and do not certify freshness of the whole fields display. No migration, physical-column alteration, default/index repair or content rewrite. Required pre-merge gates passed; intentional differences remain proposed and post-merge CI is verified separately in the feature ledger.

existing framework or storage difference.

| FS-03 | Omitted optional `constructor` field observes the inherited function and rejects `invalid_type`; own string/null accepts in both exact source and actual CMS service. | Shared upstream bug deliberately preserved in [issue35](https://github.com/sveltery/cms/issues/35). | FS-04 | Registry metadata is copied atomically to richer v3 tables; all existing content DDL/rows/IDs/defaults are retained. V4 adds the auth developer's empty identity tables. Latest markers are a positive contiguous provider sequence with rollback and verified race recovery. | Local storage mapping and explicit migration extension; supplemental Node/local D1 checks. | FS-05 | Exact pinned validation helper returns ordered issue paths/messages/bounds/formats and checks referenced targets; actual create/update uses it and exact serialize/deserialize functions. | Fidelity extension to real service boundary. Registered native issue envelopes and 17-type editor/manifest projections are dependent transport/UI work. Legacy strict input, limits/CAS/atomicity adaptations retain their existing records. |
| FS-06 | Pure generator preserves EmDash import/module augmentation text. | Source fidelity evidence only. A usable native public generated query API remains incomplete; no API compatibility or native codegen completion claim. |
| FS-07 | Pinned JSON field validation accepts unknown JavaScript values; its unchanged codec can omit nested undefined/function/symbol, coerce nonfinite numbers to null, serialize Date to a string, Map/Set to empty objects, and sparse arrays to null slots. Local shared content input accepts only recursive JSON primitives, dense arrays and plain/null-prototype objects, rejecting those unsupported values, cycles, accessors and extra non-JSON properties before create/update writes. Repeated noncyclic object references remain accepted. | Intentional stricter local input adaptation, not a source fidelity repair or new source assertion credit. Actual Node/local D1 service no-write regressions and the dependent transport's enhanced HTTP cases cover rejection. The vendored Zod generator and value codec remain unchanged; specific adaptation acceptance is not recorded. |

PR #39 review also verified pinned collection SEO derivation and timestamp touches against registry blob `da7bbcdda224dfca8d9146b2f36659ab90bcac2e`. Creation/update derive `hasSeo` from supplied supports unless explicitly overridden. Collection reorder touches every listed/cleared collection's timestamp; field deletion touches a collection only when clearing its title/date pointer, with no version bump. The existing local monotonic timestamp/CAS adaptation applies to these metadata changes so stale tokens reject. ## UX1 native shell proposal — PR #66

[The shell feature record](../../docs/admin-shell.md) and [source ledger](../../docs/admin-shell-ports.json) identify immutable EmDash 1.1.0 and Sveltery UI pins. The faithful grouping/version helpers execute nine unchanged whole source callbacks after initial import failures (zero genuine source reds). Six genuine original compiled-Svelte UI assertion reds become seven green native cases, with five Node/actual-D1/display-method cases and four registered-query/SSR/refresh/active-route cases after the native review repairs. These native cases earn no source credit.

UX1-01 replaces React/Kumo routing/sidebar/providers with base-aware Svelte links, native details/mobile controls and the existing trusted `schema:manage` display capability. This is an intentional framework/capability substitution; role filtering, icons, taxonomy/plugin navigation and the retained complete React browser callbacks remain unfinished. UX1-02 consumes immutable private/unreleased Sveltery UI Card sources and theme variables through source-copy, with scoped native CSS instead of its full Tailwind appearance. The source test-host dependency closure retains existing dependency graph entries; local npm HTTP503 failures are not passes; a normal hosted frozen install qualified the closure and the JSX/type host adaptation subsequently ran all 23 copied paired Card DOM callbacks unchanged. This is supplementary UI evidence with zero EmDash/causal source red credit. An additional native test-first browser red for stale collection labels is repaired by keeping the display query reactive and adding only its refresh to the existing schema hook; the unchanged callback passed secured59/59 at de36280 and all SSR-repaired normal/secured gates at58b448e; that evidence is historical for the later decision-document head. UX1-03 narrows the prior local Node24 range to >=24.15.0 <25 to match immutable jsdom30.1.1 engine requirements (^22.22.2 || ^24.15.0 || >=26.0.0); the PM explicitly authorized this floor after qualification, while full UX1 scope remains incomplete. Root manifest/bootstrap/docs and the copied standalone engine declaration agree; source dependencies/assertions stay unchanged. Authenticated SSR rendering, ordinary non-GET storage avoidance, authorized remote-refresh snapshots and actual relative-link selection have four original native assertion reds, zero source credit, and focused green. Kit’s trusted remote flag preserves the existing schema refresh; display href normalization precedes the unchanged pinned active matcher; no root layout/private Search graph or domain/provider mutation is included. No unavailable Input/Base component or absent feature route is advertised.

Specific decision, PM on 2026-10-03: UX1-01 is accepted only for native routing/base hrefs, the SSR query/reactive refresh, native details/mobile/skip HTML controls and trusted capability presentation. UX1-02 is accepted only for the seven copied Card parts/class/themes, scoped native CSS and recorded import/test-host adaptations. UX1-03's engine floor `>=24.15.0 <25` is accepted. Decision evidence is the [durable public PM decision and independent-review outcome record](https://github.com/sveltery/cms/pull/66#pullrequestreview-5399854986) at exact `2f05605`, actual prior-head hosted logs and [configured result5966824135](https://github.com/sveltery/cms/pull/66#issuecomment-5966824135). ## Native HTTP fixture listener lifecycle

The [Native fixture record](../../docs/runtime-port-lifecycle.md) covers a test-only initial port allocation repair. The project authority remains EmDash 1.1.0 `913cb1bb9b7f08c3ff0d258b4420e53835b6a58e`; this change copies no new upstream function or assertion and changes no production behavior. It adds three ordinary Native HTTP lifecycle callbacks with zero Source callbacks or parity credit.

The old fixture reserved a temporary net port, closed it, then bound the actual child server at that released port. The child now requests port zero directly and reports its actual trusted localhost origin before platform/Kit initialization. Parent HTTP/browser requests and real process restart use that reported origin; their existing request, credential, authorization, callback and assertion logic remains unchanged. Fixed-origin restart keeps its numeric port and can still fail if an unrelated process occupies it. The historical `EADDRINUSE` port owner remains unassigned, and the separate Node hosting helper is unchanged. No retry, origin rotation, deadline widening, protected probe or validation slicing is introduced.

The linked [whole TDD receipt](../../docs/receipts/runtime-port-lifecycle/tdd.json) records test-first Native3: two reached requested-port assertion failures and one genuine process-restart control green, followed by Native3 green after the single listener fix and after a shared-diagnostics refactor. Existing Source and Native test declarations are unchanged; full current-head bootstrap/browser execution and final reviews remain pending. PM has qualified this finite development scope; exact-head merge approval is pending and is not inferred from development qualification or the three Native greens. No intentional EmDash product-behavior difference or newly verified shared upstream bug is claimed.

| Record | Source and proposed native behavior | Evidence and decision status |
| --- | --- | --- |
| DC-01: command/framework and Worker contract | Source exposes `emdash doctor` through citty/consola and an EmDash Worker module. Native exposes real `pnpm doctor` / `node scripts/doctor.mjs` plus bundled `node build/doctor.js` and a `sveltery-doctor` package bin. | DC-02: database/schema and read-only inspection | Source opens its database and reads its full migration status, `_emdash_collections`, `users` and Source datetimes. Native explicitly opens read-only, reads the actual native registry/version records and `_cms_collections`, and counts real `_cms_auth_users` authentication identities. The whole Source datetime scanner retains its logic, pages and sample limits after finite imports/table namespaces; this command never calls its normalization/write function. Unknown/gapped native migrations fail rather than becoming a future-version fast path. | These are explicit storage/CLI substitutions, not full Source migration-runner, locking, users/profile or deployed D1 parity. Real persisted SQL and byte-unchanged database tests are registered test-first; diagnostic value assertions and current validation remain pending. Specific acceptance is not yet recorded. |
| DC-03: scheduler scope | Source doctor checks Cron/EmDash scheduled-handler pairing. Native reports static Cron/revision-maintenance pairing, validates the referenced local maintenance export, and adds an explicit warning that full scheduled publishing, cron execution and heartbeat integration remain unfinished. Node and Cloudflare wiring results remain additive with local database results. | No heartbeat row, fake maintenance implementation or private pending provider is imported. Full runtime scheduling remains a prerequisite owned by its feature. The original dashboard `npx emdash doctor` assertion remains an honestly unfulfilled native command difference. Specific acceptance is pending. |
| DC-04: TOML parse diagnostics | Pinned Source forwards `smol-toml`'s full error message, including a configuration source excerpt. Proposed native errors report invalid TOML and its actual line/column while omitting the source excerpt; JSONC already reports only its code/location. | Actual pinned parser 1.6.0 returns line 1 / column 28 and embeds the synthetic value for `main = "do-not-print-this" BROKEN`. This is an explicit proposed formatting/privacy difference, not an upstream-bug or Source causal-credit claim. The thirteen-case Native baseline fails at missing CLI startup; later privacy/location assertions are unreached. Specific acceptance is pending. |
